Los Angeles County, California

Raising kids in Los Angeles, Long Beach or anywhere else in Los Angeles County? Here is who to call close to home. To apply for public housing or a Section 8 voucher, start with Housing Authority of the City of Los Angeles. Free legal help with custody, child support, protection from abuse or an eviction comes from Neighborhood Legal Services of Los Angeles County. Expecting, or have a little one? You will find the health department, home visits for new parents, breastfeeding support and free diapers near you. Low-cost clinics, free evaluations for your child and Head Start are below too. Pick what you need. Free, private, no sign-up.

Another county in California · Find yours by ZIP code

Need help right now?

Free, and answered any hour. If you or your child are in danger, call 911.

  • Call 800-799-7233Domestic violence hotline
  • Call 988Suicide and crisis line
  • Call 800-422-4453Child abuse hotline

Cannot talk out loud? Text START to 88788 for the domestic violence hotline, or text 988. More ways to get help now

Nothing you type here is sent or saved.

Christmas help

Toys for Tots for Los Angeles County, and Christmas help across California3 campaigns

Toys for Tots campaigns named for Los Angeles County, or with their address there. Sign-ups close early. If one has closed, dial 211 and ask what Christmas help is still open. More Christmas help in California · Christmas help everywhere

Toys for Tots: Palmdale

Named for Los Angeles County, or with its address there. New toys for children whose families cannot afford Christmas gifts.

Campaign website

More about this: Toys for Tots: Palmdale

Marine Toys for Tots

The campaign's website says which towns it serves, its dates, and whether it takes requests from families or only through local charities and agencies.

Next step: Open the website and its Request a Toy page. If requests have closed, use its Contact page and ask which local groups are giving out its toys.

Public housing and Section 8

Where to apply in Los Angeles County for public housing and rent vouchers20 agencies

Next step: Call and ask whether the waiting list is open, how to apply, and whether they serve where you live. You can be on more than one agency's list. If you are homeless or escaping violence, ask whether that moves you up the list.

Housing Authority of the City of Los Angeles

Office in Los Angeles, CA. Public housing and Housing Choice Vouchers (Section 8).

Call 833-422-5248

More about this: Housing Authority of the City of Los Angeles

Public housing agency, funded by HUD

2600 Wilshire Blvd, Los Angeles, CA 90057

By HUD's count, about 6,000 public housing homes and about 53,456 vouchers.

Office in Alhambra, CA. Public housing and Housing Choice Vouchers (Section 8).

More about this: Los Angeles County Development Authority

Public housing agency, funded by HUD

700 W Main St, Alhambra, CA 91801

By HUD's count, about 2,962 public housing homes and about 27,201 vouchers.

Housing counselors

Free or low-cost help with rent, eviction and mortgages4 counselors

Next step: Call and say what is happening: rent you cannot pay, an eviction notice, a shutoff, or a mortgage you are behind on. Ask whether there is a fee.

Shalom Center for T.R.E.E. of Life

In Los Angeles County. Free or low-cost help from a HUD-approved counselor with mortgage trouble, budgeting and buying a home.

Call 213-380-3700Website

More about this: Shalom Center for T.R.E.E. of Life

HUD-approved housing counseling agency

2975 Wilshire Blvd, Los Angeles, CA 90010

Counseling is offered online, by phone and by video.

Also helps in Korean.

New Economics for Women

In Los Angeles County. Free or low-cost help from a HUD-approved counselor with mortgage trouble, budgeting and buying a home.

Call 213-483-2060Website

More about this: New Economics for Women

HUD-approved housing counseling agency

303 Loma Dr, Los Angeles, CA 90017

Counseling is offered in person, online, by phone and by video.

Also helps in Spanish.

Help for moms and babies

Health department, home visits for new parents, breastfeeding support and diapers in or near Los Angeles County18 places

Health department

Next step: Call and ask what they offer for moms and babies, such as WIC, shots, a nurse visit after birth or help finding prenatal care.

County of Los Angeles, Public Health

In Los Angeles, CA. Your local health department: shots for children, WIC in many places, pregnancy tests and prenatal referrals, and help finding a doctor.

Call 323-563-4068

More about this: County of Los Angeles, Public Health

Public health department or clinic

1522 E 102nd St, Los Angeles, CA 90002

Free legal help

Legal aid that serves Los Angeles County1 program

Next step: Call and say what is happening and any date you have to meet, such as a court date or the deadline on an eviction notice. If they cannot take your case, ask who can.

Neighborhood Legal Services of Los Angeles County

Free legal help for people with low incomes, with problems such as custody, child support, protection from abuse and eviction.

Call 800-433-6251Website

More about this: Neighborhood Legal Services of Los Angeles County

Legal aid program funded by the Legal Services Corporation

It serves Los Angeles County and the rest of its area, and can tell you if another program is the one for you.

Federal data as of November 16, 2026, not confirmed by phone. Sources: 1 2

Health centers in Los Angeles County611 places

🧩

Free evaluation for your child

School districts in Los Angeles County, for children 3 and up78 districts

From age 3, children can get special education free through the public schools, even before kindergarten. If you think your child has a delay or disability, call your school district and ask for an evaluation. If the district decides not to evaluate, it has to tell you why in writing. For a child under 3, ask early intervention in California instead.

Next step: Call and ask for the special education office, or preschool special education for a child under 5. Say you would like your child evaluated, then make the same request in writing, with the date, and keep a copy.
